# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Re-tracked blobs
#
# These were installed by hand-written entries in the generated
# vendor/xiaomi/dada makefiles while having no entry here, so re-running
# extract-files.py against a stock ROM silently dropped them.
#
# MAKE_COPY_RULE_ONLY is deliberate. They were previously emitted as
# PRODUCT_COPY_FILES, and four of them -- libgralloc.qti, libgralloccore,
# libgrallocutils and vendor.qti.hardware.display.allocator-service -- share
# their module names with hardware/qcom-caf/sm8750/display/hal/gralloc. Turning
# them into Soong modules would make Soong fail with 'found in multiple
# namespaces'. Copy rules keep the existing behaviour byte for byte while
# making the files reproducible from a stock ROM.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

odm/lib64/libqc_hal.so;MAKE_COPY_RULE_ONLY|385dfcad2509e451855d2ed1aedee426e5b7d1ce
vendor/bin/hw/vendor.qti.hardware.display.allocator-service;MAKE_COPY_RULE_ONLY|65d334d59596127c75942a768659981d680c1cdf
vendor/etc/init/vendor.qti.hardware.display.allocator-service.rc;MAKE_COPY_RULE_ONLY|4bc1178af72d1afc8daa38cc98fbb874d7e467d7
vendor/etc/vintf/manifest/vendor.qti.hardware.display.allocator-service.xml|d6adf0203bf2419163fd673f46a7391f69abf272
vendor/lib64/libc++.so;MAKE_COPY_RULE_ONLY|4cae8b928cfb9eb8958ccdc613aa81600c0b99f9
vendor/lib64/libgralloc.qti.so;MAKE_COPY_RULE_ONLY|51e3744cf66b4af7cf0594baf5ea0f0293774e5b
vendor/lib64/libgralloccore.so;MAKE_COPY_RULE_ONLY|5502b0e33f7ae50ef0006213b6f89f9a0a3f5c05
vendor/lib64/libgrallocutils.so;MAKE_COPY_RULE_ONLY|920674204fb71a3c00e1ecc88efc183bb2757556
